- **qifei-proposal-v2**: 公司提案总控：把品牌需求做成有观点、能打动评审、可视觉化交付的竞争性提案或提案模块（比稿、品牌提案、营销方案 PPT、飞书提案母稿、提案视觉与讲稿、导出 PNG/PDF/PPTX）。按项目阶段渐进加载规则，内置策略、内容、视觉、评审与导出门禁，支持 Codex、API Agent 与本地运行环境。也用于品牌手册、品牌视觉画册这类以视觉表现为主的演示文件（视觉表现型路径：整页生图、文字一并画入）。用户说“做提案”“比稿”“写方案 PPT”“继续这个提案项目”“做提案里的某个模块”“做品牌手册”“做品牌画册”时使用。不用于通用文档排版、单张海报或与提案无关的 PPT 美化。已有确认的页面大纲和视觉方向、只需要出页面时，用 proposal-ppt-production。 ([chenxupeng1990-eng/qifei-proposal-skill](https://github.com/chenxupeng1990-eng/qifei-proposal-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/qifei-proposal-v2) · ★113)

- **qifeng-writing**: 诊断、创作和优化中文 X 推文、Thread、长推、公众号文章、X Article、观点评论、知识文章、个人经历与推广文案。先识别问题层级和编辑边界，再选择最少且足够的中文创作技法进行局部或全文修改。用户只要求分析时不得改稿；只要求局部时不得改动其他部分。标题专改、排版发布、纯资料研究、小说诗歌不属于本 Skill 的主要范围。 ([adunext/human-video-knowledge](https://github.com/adunext/human-video-knowledge/tree/HEAD/upstream/Yuriloll__qifeng-writing) · ★56)

- **qiime-file-format-parsing**: Use when the input consists of QIIME-format principal coordinates files (including jackknifed replicate PCoA files) and a metadata mapping file that must be converted into per-sample coordinates and category memberships for 3D visualization or recoloring/show/hide controllers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/qiime-file-format-parsing))
- **qiime-mapping-file-metadata-integration**: Use when a QIIME-generated principal coordinates file must be explored interactively in 3D with sample metadata (mapping file columns) driving coloring, visibility, and axes for thousands of microbial community samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/qiime-mapping-file-metadata-integration))
- **qiime-principal-coordinates-input-processing**: Use when input data are QIIME principal coordinates files plus metadata mapping files (e.g., 5,740 samples and 120 metadata columns) that must be coupled for interactive 3D ordination with discrete or gradient coloring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/qiime-principal-coordinates-input-processing))
- **qiime2-16s-microbiome**: DADA2 amplicon sequence variant (ASV) dereplication, Alpha/Beta diversity index calculation, SILVA taxonomy classification, and 3D PCoA projections. ([YuliaNuzhnenko/bioinformatics-agent-skills](https://github.com/YuliaNuzhnenko/bioinformatics-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/qiime2-16s-microbiome) · ★10)
- **qiime2-alpha-diversity-table-handling**: Use when alpha diversity metrics have been computed with QIIME2's diversity module and need to be aggregated per dataset, combined with metadata, and visualized/compared (e.g., correlations, boxplots per project). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-alpha-diversity-table-handling))
- **qiime2-amplicon**: Processes paired-end 16S amplicon reads into QIIME 2 ASVs and taxonomy with retained artifact provenance. Checks paired FASTQ manifests, primer orientation diagnostics, predicted post-trimming overlap, sample IDs, runtime versions and read retention, and guides selection of compatible taxonomic classifiers. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/tree/HEAD/skills/science-pack/qiime2-amplicon) · ★6 · 50 copies)
- **qiime2-artifact-format-conversion**: Use when a reconstructed SSU sequences FASTA (e.g. metagenome_SSU_sequences.fasta from RiboTaxa's EMIRGE/MetaRib assembly) must be converted into a QIIME 2 .qza artifact before classify-sklearn classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-format-conversion))
- **qiime2-artifact-generation**: Use when raw demultiplexed Illumina amplicon FASTQ data (e.g. a Lake Erie 16S rRNA dataset) plus a Tourmaline configuration file must be converted into QIIME 2 feature-table, representative-sequence, taxonomy, and diversity artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-generation))
- **qiime2-artifact-handling**: Use when you have raw mass-spectrometry files (MGF, BIOM, mzXML, mzML) or feature abundance tables from external tools (MZmine2, peak detection software) and need to convert them into QIIME 2's typed artifact format before running downstream plugins like q2-qemistree, diversity analyses, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/qiime2-artifact-handling) · 2 copies)
- **qiime2-artifact-import**: Use when you hold a feature table (BIOM/TSV) or denoised count data that must become a QIIME 2 FeatureTable[Frequency] .qza artifact before running qiime diversity alpha or feature-table summarize/rarefy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-import--44766eb4b05ac2e5))
- **qiime2-artifact-import**: Use when downloaded reference sequences (FASTA) or taxonomy files must be converted into typed QIIME 2 artifacts (FeatureData[Sequence], FeatureData[Taxonomy]) before classifier training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-import--a9b98430d1674072))
- **qiime2-artifact-import-export**: Use when starting from an MZmine2-produced MGF and quant/feature table (e.g. MassIVE MSV000082969 Cheetah Quant table) and you need typed QIIME 2 artifacts (FeatureTable[Frequency], MassSpectrometryFeatures) as Qemistree inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/qiime2-artifact-import-export))
- **qiime2-artifact-inspection**: Use when you need to verify that a QIIME 2 artifact (e.g., a Chemical Feature Tree from q2-qemistree, a FeatureTable[Frequency], or a Phylogeny[Rooted] object) has been correctly produced, before using it as input to downstream analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/qiime2-artifact-inspection) · 3 copies)
- **qiime2-artifact-processing**: Use when demultiplexed paired-end amplicon reads (e.g., COI from mock communities or bat guano) must be imported to QIIME 2, trimmed, denoised or clustered into feature tables, and compared for unexpected-sequence error rates under alternative filtering stringencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-processing))
- **qiime2-artifact-qc-summary-extraction**: Use when comparing multiple Tourmaline/QIIME 2 runs (e.g., a DADA2 truncation-length sweep) and you need per-run representative sequence length distributions and taxonomy-assigned read counts aggregated into a summary table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-qc-summary-extraction))
- **qiime2-artifact-serialization**: Use when habitat-specific taxonomic weight vectors computed by q2-clawback's generate_class_weights must be persisted as QIIME 2 FeatureWeights artifacts (class_weights.qza) or exchanged for downstream classifier training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-serialization))
- **qiime2-artifact-visualization-interpretation**: Use when QIIME 2 distance matrices and ordinations exist (or a .qzv report is produced) and you must inspect Emperor plots or extract PERMANOVA pseudo-F/p-values to judge whether samples group by a metadata column. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-visualization-interpretation))
- **qiime2-artifact-workflow**: Use when classifying 16S amplicon sequence variants against Greengenes 99% (or Silva 138.1) reference data in QIIME 2 and you want environment-specific (e.g., EMPO 3 habitat) taxonomic class weights rather than the uniform-weight assumption. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-artifact-workflow))
- **qiime2-classifier-inference**: Use when raw or denoised 16S rRNA amplicon representative sequences need taxonomic assignment under multiple reference databases (Greengenes, SILVA, NCBI RefSeq) to compare predicted vs. expected taxonomic compositions, e.g. on mock community benchmark data from Zenodo record 7051556. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/qiime2-classifier-inference))
- **qiime2-classifier-workflow-execution**: Use when a marker-gene amplicon dataset must be taxonomically classified in QIIME 2, and the goal is to benchmark species-level classification with environment-specific (bespoke) class weights against genus-level uniform-weight classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-classifier-workflow-execution))
- **qiime2-classify-sklearn-configuration**: Use when reconstructed or clustered SSU rRNA FASTA sequences (e.g. EMIRGE/MetaRib output from metagenomics reads) must be assigned taxonomy down to genus/species level via a naive-Bayes trained classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/qiime2-classify-sklearn-configuration))
- **qiime2-diversity-module-execution**: Use when alpha diversity metrics (richness, dominance, phylogenetic, information categories) must be recalculated from QIIME2-processed feature tables, e.g. to reproduce or validate metric behavior on rarefied vs non-rarefied stool-sample datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/qiime2-diversity-module-execution))
- **qiime2-feature-classifier-workflow**: Use when full-length or near-full-length SSU rRNA sequences (e.g. 97%-clustered reconstructions in SSU_sequences.fasta) need taxonomic lineages plus per-sequence relative abundance from metagenomic reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/qiime2-feature-classifier-workflow))
- **qiime2-microbiome-analyzer**: QIIME2 microbiome analysis skill for 16S rRNA profiling and diversity analysis ([a5c-ai/babysitter](https://github.com/a5c-ai/babysitter/tree/HEAD/library/specializations/domains/science/bioinformatics/skills/qiime2-microbiome-analyzer) · ★1.9k)
- **qiime2-naive-bayes-classifier-training**: Use when benchmarking 16S rRNA taxonomic classifiers on a common reference database (e.g. Greengenes2 2024.09 FASTA) and a held-out test set, to obtain a naive-Bayes baseline comparable to DADA2, SINTAX, and Kraken 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/qiime2-naive-bayes-classifier-training))
- **qiime2-qzv-visualization-parsing**: Use when a QIIME 2 run's .qzv outputs (e.g., observed_features_group_significance.qzv, alpha_rarefaction.qzv) must be parsed to extract per-sample values or curve data for scripted validation or downstream analysis.
